# Graph Report - ObsidianVault  (2026-07-24)

## Corpus Check
- 9236 files · ~16,771,772 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 50137 nodes · 97449 edges · 3889 communities (2503 shown, 1386 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4507 edges (avg confidence: 0.7)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `ee85bbfb`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

## Community Hubs (Navigation)

> **2507 community links** previously pointed to non-existent files (Graphify bug — it doesn't generate separate community files). This block has been rewritten to in-file anchors.
> Real community detail headings live in the **Communities** section below.
